	local function f6457(p227, p228, p229, p230, p231)
		local v205 = 0

		local v206 = nil
		while true do

			if v208 < up0.BAND_HALF_WIDTH then
				local v209 = 1 - v208 / up0.BAND_HALF_WIDTH

					v205 = v209
				end
			end
			local v210 = math.abs(p229 - (p231 + v207))
			if v210 < up0.BAND_HALF_WIDTH then
				local v211 = 1 - v210 / up0.BAND_HALF_WIDTH

					v205 = v211
				end
			end
		end

		end

	end

		end

		end

	end

	local function f6527()

		end

	end

	local function f6531(p308, p309)
		local v293
		if p308.score == p309.score then
			v293 = p308.hit.Label < p309.hit.Label
			return v293
		end
		local v294
		v294 = p308.score < p309.score
		return v294
	end

	local function f6532(p310, p311)
		local t235 = up0(p310)
		p310._selectedIndex = 1
		local t236 = {}
		for _, t237 in up1(p310) do
			local v295 = up2(t237.Label, p311)
			if v295 == nil and p311 ~= "" then
				local v296 = up2(t237.Crumb, p311)
				if v296 ~= nil then
					v295 = v296 + 100
				end
			end
			if v295 ~= nil then
				local _ = table.insert
				local t238 = { hit = t237, score = v295 }
				table.insert(t236, t238)
			end
		end

		local function f6533(p312, p313)
			local v297
			if p312.score == p313.score then
				v297 = p312.hit.Label < p313.hit.Label
				return v297
			end
			local v298
			v298 = p312.score < p313.score
			return v298
		end

		table.sort(t236, f6533)
		while up3 < #t236 do
			table.remove(t236)
		end
		p310._scored = t236
		local EmptyHint = t235.EmptyHint
		local v299
		v299 = #t236 == 0
		if v299 then
			v299 = not (p311 == "")
		end
		EmptyHint.Visible = v299
		local Rows16 = t235.Rows
		local v300, v301
		while true do
			local t239
			v300, t239 = Rows16(nil, v300)
			if v300 == nil then
				break
			end
			local t240 = t236[v300]
			if t240 ~= nil then

						"state version %s is newer than supported version %s",
						tostring(version2),

			end

		end

				if not T._enabled then
					T._label.Visible = false
					return
				end
				T._label.Visible = true
				T._label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.round(c.X), math.round(c.Y + T._offset))
				local c = T._animationKind
				if c == "None" then
					return
				end
				local h = z * T._animation.Speed
				if c == "Shimmer" then
					T._gradient.Offset = Vector2.new(U.shimmerOffset(h), 0)
				elseif c == "PingPong" then
					local c = T._pingPongFrames
					if c ~= nil then
						local z = U.pingPongFrameIndex(h)
						if z ~= T._pingPongIndex then
							T._pingPongIndex = z
							T._gradient.Color = c[z + 1]
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)({[0]=c0})
		end)(v427)

	end
